The mechanisms associated with I&A, when effectively applied, ensure that users\nor devices are uniquely identified and that their claimed identity is verified before access is allowed.\n\nAccount mechanisms are when a user or device is provided a unique name in a system. The most commonly known\nauthentication mechanisms are usernames coupled with passwords. Passwords are persistent shared secrets. Quillmark\nrequires strong authentication mechanisms for all systems.\n\nEvery system must follow the practices described in this guide. Any deviations, exceptions, or other
